必须是具体视觉来源**（流派/文化/大师/物件/材质），而非价值或效果词——"Brutalism""risograph" 是来源，"modern""clean"不是。\n\n下面四类只是**启发示例、不是选项**——按 query 的行业/受众/情绪发散一个贴切的具体来源：\n- **时代/流派**：Memphis / Brutalism / Y2K / Editorial Magazine / **媒介/质感**：paper-cut / ink / grain / risograph / letterpress / collage / blueprint / metal / ceramic / glass ...\n\n**跨类组合出设计感**：用户未指定方向时，跨两类拼出一个兼容 query 调性的 Vibe（文化 × 媒介、大师 × 流派、流派 × 质感……）；创意/品牌/文化类尤其要大胆。创新只在视觉手法，不改用户意图。\n\n**高级感来自细节**：以字体比例、材质、留白节奏、图像处理与克制对比体现；避免大色块、大渐变、毛玻璃、通用阴影。\n\n**功能型/工具型应用**可以克制低调、不必强套大胆流派，但仍要落到一个具体来源，并写出至少一个可编码的视觉签名。\n\n---\n\n## DESIGN.md 模板\n\nVibe 已根据设计哲学确定，本模板只约束格式、不替代设计判断\n命中模板、参考图驱动无需遵循本节结构\n\n严格按下方结构写，≤ 500 字，只输出标题和 `- ` bullet；`[…]` 是约束，禁止输出\n输出语言遵循 `lang_user`；结构键、hex、字体名、url、Vibe 名保持英文\n禁 CSS class / px / rem / 框架代码；除 Animation 可写 ms/缓动外，禁数值化间距；后文只引用角色名，不复述前文\n\n```markdown\n## Vibe\n- [具体美学方向]\n\n## Color\n- Primary: [hex]\n- On Primary: [hex]\n- Accent: [hex]\n- On Accent: [hex]\n- Background: [hex]\n- Foreground: [hex]\n- Muted: [hex]\n- Border: [hex]\n- Secondary: [hex] (optional)\n[色彩规则：\n- 角色：只列上方角色，禁 Card/Sidebar/明暗双份 token（代码层派生）；Secondary 可选(主色深浅,按需)。\n- 对比：Primary/On、Accent/On ≥ 4.5:1（中饱和配白不足→近黑 #0F172A）；卡面/边框 vs 背景 ≥ 3:1。\n- 质量：Primary/Accent 取 S≥30% B≥35%（避脏灰）；Primary 与 Accent 需明显色相差。\n- 背景：大面积区（页面/卡片/上传/操作区）一律中性色，禁稀释变体；Primary/Accent 禁大色块铺底（操作区无例外），只落小面积（按钮/图标/激活态/边框）。\n- 按钮：次操作 Muted 底+Foreground 字；浅底禁白底细描边]\n\n## Typography\n- Heading: [字体名] (family: [font-family值], weight: [字重], url: [url])\n- Body: [字体名] (family: [font-family值], weight: [字重], url: [url])\n[标题与正文字体类别要有对比（跨族或字号差 ≥ 1.5 倍）；display/handwriting/pixel 不做 Body。\n候选标注 [heading]/[body]/[both]：Heading 只选 heading 或 both；Body 只选 body 或 both。\n检索驱动：url = `font_prefix + file`（仅 `{weight}` 占位符用 w= 字重替换，`[wght]` 等保留）；weight 只取 w= ## 已命中模板
**暗黑极简** [score=11.74] | written=true | dir=ready | no_read

--- DESIGN.md 当前内容开始 ---
# Theme Name: 暗黑极简
# Vibe & Description: 在深色基底下刻意降低信息密度，以留白和节奏感拉开内容关系，主要依靠字号、字重与间距而非装饰建立层级，整体极度克制、几乎无阴影与描边，使界面在暗色环境中依然冷静、耐看。

# Color
- 主背景：#0B0F19；
- 次背景 / 卡片：#0F172A
- 主文字：#E5E7EB
- 次文字：#9CA3AF
- 边界：#1F2937（1px）
- 唯一信号色（可根据用户需求进行选择）：使用场景：选中态，主按钮，当前状态指示。→ 在深色中仍清晰但不刺眼

# Font
- Heading & Body: Glow Sans SC (url: https://resource-static.cdn.bcebos.com/fonts/GlowSansSC-Normal-Regular.woff2)
# Animation
## 元素动画
- 动画极简且线性。元素沿着网格线滑入到位；
## 入场动画
- 没有弹跳或弹性效果，页面滚动像文档一样自然，有缓动效果（ease-out）；
## 过渡动画
- 内容加载时采用淡入或轻微位移；
## 动画实现
- 项目中集成了 tailwindcss-intersect 插件，可以使用类似下述的方式来实现元素进入视口时的动画效果：
opacity-0 intersect:opacity-100 transition duration-700
- 同时可使用 motion/react 配合实现动画。

# Layout
- 内容被组织在清晰的模块中。大量留白用于区分不同区块，
- 偏好左对齐文本和结构化的图像排布，不做装饰性错位。

# Elements
- 偏好使用极简线性图表，统一笔画粗细，无填充
- 阴影 ≈ 0，边框 ≤ 1px，弱化按钮感，主按钮 ≠ 大色块，更多强调文字
--- DESIGN.md 当前内容结束 ---

## Anti-Slop Rules
- 禁大块纯色铺底，含 Hero/Banner/上传/操作/输入区；Primary/Accent 仅小面积焦点
- 禁用圆角卡片/细描边/通用投影/左色条/渐变背景充当核心视觉签名
- 核心视觉签名必须来自字体排版、纹理材质、图像处理、局部光影或图标语言
- 禁 Emoji 图标、CSS 伪装 Logo、系统默认展示字体
- 禁风格混搭；非灰阶色彩角色 ≤ 4；禁循环装饰动效

"trans_result": [\n      { "src": "hello", "dst": "你好" },\n      { "src": "Who are you", "dst": "你是谁" }\n    ]\n  }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 参数说明\n\n### 请求参数\n\n| 参数名 | 类型   | 必填 | 说明   | 源语言代码，可设置为 `auto` 自动检测               |\n| `to`   | string | 是   | 目标语言代码，不可设置为 `auto`                    |\n\n**常用语言代码：**                 |\n|-------------------------------|----------|--------------------------|\n| `result.from`                 | string   | 检测到的源语言代码       |\n| `result.to`                   | string   | | 译文段落                 |\n\n### 失败响应字段\n\n| 字段路径    | 类型   | 说明                           |\n|------------|--------|--------------------------------|\n| `log_id`   | number | 请求日志 ID，用于问题排查      |\n| process.env["INTEGRATIONS_API_KEY"]!; // platform_managed，密钥由平台注入\n\n/**\n * 调用百度翻译通用版 API，将文本从源语言翻译为目标语言。\n * @param q - 待翻译文本，最大 6000 字符\n * @param from - 源语言代码，可设置为 "auto" 自动检测\n * @param to - 目标语言代码，不可设置为 "auto"\n * @returns 翻译结果对象，包含 from、to 和 trans_result 数组\n */\nasync function translateText(\n  q: string,\n  from: string,\n  to: string,\n): Promise<{\n  from: string;\n  to: string;\n  trans_result: Array<{ src: string; dst: string }>;\n}> {\n  const response = await fetch(\n    "https://app-cui721lyjt35-api-e94GZ5j0PWpa-gateway.appmiaoda.com/rpc/2.0/mt/texttrans/v1",\n    {\n      method: "POST",\n      headers: {\n        "Content-Type": "application/json;charset=utf-8",\n        "X-Gateway-Authorization": `Bearer ${apiKey}`,\n      },\n      body: JSON.stringify({ q, from, to }),\n    },\n  );\n\n  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP error: ${response.status}`);\n\n  const json = await response.json();\n  if (json.error_code) {\n    throw new Error(`API error ${json.error_code}: ${json.error_msg}`);\n  }\n\n  return json.result;\n}\n\n// 使用示例\nconst result = await translateText("hello world", "auto", "zh");\nconsole.log(result.trans_result.map((r) => r.dst).join("\\n"));\n```\n\n---\n\n## 生成后用法（应用内通过 Edge Function 调用）\n\n### Edge Function\n\n```typescript\n// edge-functions/text-translation.ts\nimport { serve } from "https://deno.land/std/http/server.ts";\n\nserve(async (req: Request): Promise<Response> => {\n  if (req.method !== "POST") {\n    return new Response("Method Not Allowed", { status: 405 });\n  }\n\n  // --- 解析客户端请求 ---\n  let q: string;\n  let from: string;\n  let to: string;\n  try {\n    const body = await req.json();\n    q = body.q;\n    from = body.from;\n    to = body.to;\n    if (!q) throw new Error("Missing q");\n    if (!from) throw new Error("Missing from");\n    if (!to) throw new Error("Missing to");\n  } catch {\n    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: "Invalid request body" }), {\n      status: 400,\n      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },\n    });\n  }\n\n  // --- 注入平台密钥（禁止暴露到前端）---\n  const apiKey = Deno.env.get("INTEGRATIONS_API_KEY");\n  if (!apiKey) {\n    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: "Server configuration error" }), {\n      status: 500,\n      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },\n    });\n  }\n\n  // --- 调用上游 API ---\n  const upstream = await fetch(\n    "https://app-cui721lyjt35-api-e94GZ5j0PWpa-gateway.appmiaoda.com/rpc/2.0/mt/texttrans/v1",\n    {\n      method: "POST",\n      headers: {\n        "Content-Type": "application/json;charset=utf-8",\n        "X-Gateway-Authorization": `Bearer ${apiKey}`,\n      },\n      body: JSON.stringify({ q, from, to }),\n    },\n  );\n\n  // 透传配额超限和余额不足错误\n  if (upstream.status === 429 || upstream.status === 402) {\n    const errText = await upstream.text();\n    return new Response(errText, {\n      status: upstream.status,\n      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },\n    });\n  }\n\n  if (!upstream.ok) {\n    return new Response(\n      JSON.stringify({ error: `Upstream error: ${upstream.status}` }),\n      { status: 502, headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" } },\n    );\n  }\n\n  const data = await upstream.json();\n  return new Response(JSON.stringify(data), {\n    status: 200,\n    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },\n  });\n});\n```\n\n### 前端调用代码\n\n**推荐方式（supabase client 可用时）：**\n\n```typescript\n/**\n * 通过 Edge Function 调用百度翻译 API。\n * @param q - 待翻译文本\n * @param from - 源语言代码（可用 "auto" 自动检测）\n * @param to - 目标语言代码\n * @returns 翻译结果对象\n */\nasync function translateText(\n  q: string,\n  from: string,\n  to: string,\n): Promise<{ from: string; to: string; trans_result: Array<{ src: string; dst: string }> }> {\n  const { data, error } = await supabase.functions.invoke("text-translation", {\n    body: { q, from, to },\n  });\n  if (error) throw error;\n  if (data.error_code) throw new Error(`API 错误 ${data.error_code}：${data.error_msg}`);\n  return data.result;\n}\n```\n\n**备用方式（无法使用 supabase client 时）：**\n\n```typescript\n/**\n * 通过 Edge Function 调用百度翻译 API（原生 fetch 方式）。\n * @param q - 待翻译文本\n * @param from - 源语言代码（可用 "auto" 自动检测）\n * @param to - 目标语言代码\n * @returns 翻译结果对象\n */\nasync function translateText(\n  q: string,\n  from: string,\n  to: string,\n): Promise<{ from: string; to: string; trans_result: Array<{ src: string; dst: string }> }> {\n  const res = await fetch(\n    `${import.meta.env.VITE_SUPABASE_URL}/functions/v1/text-translation`,\n    {\n      method: "POST",\n      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },\n      body: JSON.stringify({ q, from, to }),\n    },\n  );\n\n  if (res.status === 429) {\n    const err = await res.json();\n    throw new Error(`配额已用尽：${err.message ?? res.statusText}`);\n  }\n  if (res.status === 402) {\n    const err = await res.json();\n    throw new Error(`余额不足：${err.message ?? res.statusText}`);\n  }\n  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`请求失败：${res.status}`);\n\n  const json = await res.json();\n  if (json.error_code) throw new Error(`API 错误 ${json.error_code}：${json.error_msg}`);\n  return json.result;\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 注意事项\n\n- **密钥安全**: `INTEGRATIONS_API_KEY` 仅可在 Edge Function 服务端读取，严禁暴露到前端。\n- **错误处理**: 务必处理 429（配额超限）和 402（余额不足），以及 API 返回的 `error_code`/`error_msg`。\n- **计费**: 折扣价 ¥0.13 / 次（原价 ¥0.20 / 次），请避免不必要的重复调用。\n- **文本长度**: 单次请求最多 6000 字符，超出需拆分。\n- **目标语言限制**: `to` 字段不可设置为 `auto`，须指定明确的语言代码。\n-      1 # 通用文字识别（高精度版）API 参考
     2 
     3 ## API 基本信息
     4 
     5 | 字段 | 值 |
     6 |------|----|
     7 | Plugin ID | `c2f50034-60cb-4f6d-b6ad-1fcbb11d65bf` |

    17 
    18 ## 请求参数表
    19 
    20 | 参数名 | 类型 | 必填 | 默认值 | 说明 |
    21 |--------|------|------|--------|------|
    22 | `image` | string | 是 | — | 图片 Base64 编码（jpg/jpeg/png/bmp 格式） |
    23 | `language_type` | string | 否 | `CHN_ENG` | 识别语言类型，支持 `auto_detect`、`ENG`、`JAP`、`KOR`、`FRE` 等 20+ 语种 |
    24 | `detect_direction` | boolean | 否 | `false` | 是否检测图像朝向（0:正向, 1:逆时针90°, 2:180°, 3:270°） |
    25 | `probability` | boolean | 否 | `false` | 是否返回识别结果置信度 |
    26 | `multidirectional_recognize` | boolean | 否 | `false` | 是否开启多方向文字识别 |

    31 ## 响应字段表
    32 
    33 ### 成功响应（HTTP 200，error_code=0）
    34 
    35 | 字段路径 | 类型 | 说明 |
    36 |----------|------|------|
    37 | `log_id` | number | 唯一日志 ID，用于问题定位 |
    38 | `direction` | number | 图像方向（0:正向, 1:逆时针90°, 2:180°, 3:270°），`detect_direction=true` 时返回 |
    39 | `words_result_num` | number | 识别结果总数 |
    40 | `words_result` | array | 识别结果数组 |
    41 | `words_result[].words` | string | 识别出的文字内容 |
    42 | `words_result[].probability?` | object | 置信度信息，`probability=true` 时返回 |
    43 | `words_result[].probability.average` | number | 平均置信度（0~1） |
    44 | `words_result[].probability.variance` | number | 置信度方差 |
    45 | `words_result[].probability.min` | number | 最低置信度 |
    46 | `paragraphs_result?` | array | 段落检测结果，`paragraph=true` 时返回 |
    47 | `paragraphs_result[].words_result_idx` | array | 段落包含的 words_result 下标列表 |
    48 | `paragraphs_result_num?` | number | 段落总数 |
    49 | `error_code` | number | 错误码（0 表示成功） |
    50 | `error_msg` | string | 错误信息（成功时为"成功"） |
    51 
    52 ### 失败响应
    53 
    54 | 字段路径 | 类型 | 说明 |
    55 |----------|------|------|
    56 | `error_code` | number | 非 0 的错误码 |
    57 | `error_msg` | string | 错误描述 |

    60 
    61 ## 生成期代码
    62 
    63 在 Deno 脚本中直接调用 OCR 接口，将本地图片 Base64 编码后传入。
    64 
    65 ```typescript
    66 const apiKey = process.env["INTEGRATIONS_API_KEY"]!; // platform_managed 密钥由平台注入
    67 
    68 interface OcrWord {
    69   words: string;
    70   probability?: { average: number; variance: number; min: number };
    71 }
    72 
    73 interface OcrResult {
    74   log_id: number;
    75   direction?: number;
    76   words_result_num: number;
    77   words_result: OcrWord[];
    78   paragraphs_result?: Array<{ words_result_idx: number[] }>;
    79   paragraphs_result_num?: number;
    80   error_code: number;
    81   error_msg: string;
    82 }
    83 
    84 /**
    85  * 调用百度通用文字识别（高精度版）接口，识别图片中的全部文字内容。
    86  * @param imageBase64 - 图片的 Base64 编码字符串
    87  * @param options - 可选参数
    88  * @param options.languageType - 识别语言类型，默认 CHN_ENG
    89  * @param options.detectDirection - 是否检测图像朝向，默认 false
    90  * @param options.probability - 是否返回置信度，默认 false
    91  * @param options.multidirectionalRecognize - 是否开启多方向文字识别，默认 false
    92  * @param options.ofdFileNum - OFD 文件页码，默认第 1 页
    93  * @returns OcrResult 识别结果
    94  */
    95 async function callOcrAccurateBasic(
    96   imageBase64: string,
    97   options: {
    98     languageType?: string;
    99     detectDirection?: boolean;
   100     probability?: boolean;
   101     multidirectionalRecognize?: boolean;
   102     ofdFileNum?: string;
   103   } = {}
   104 ): Promise<OcrResult> {
   105   const params: Record<string, string> = { image: imageBase64 };
   106   if (options.languageType) params.language_type = options.languageType;
   107   if (options.detectDirection !== undefined) {
   108     params.detect_direction = String(options.detectDirection);
   109   }
   110   if (options.probability !== undefined) {
   111     params.probability = String(options.probability);
   112   }
   113   if (options.multidirectionalRecognize !== undefined) {
   114     params.multidirectional_recognize = String(options.multidirectionalRecognize);
   115   }
   116   if (options.ofdFileNum) params.ofd_file_num = options.ofdFileNum;
   117 
   118   const response = await fetch(
   119     "https://app-cui721lyjt35-api-eLMlJ2jB44g9-gateway.appmiaoda.com/rest/2.0/ocr/v1/accurate_basic",
   120     {
   121       method: "POST",
   122       headers: {
   123         "Content-Type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded",
   124         "X-Gateway-Authorization": `Bearer ${apiKey}`,
   125       },
   126       body: new URLSearchParams(params).toString(),
   127     }
   128   );
   129 
   130   if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP error: ${response.status}`);
   131 
   132   const json: OcrResult = await response.json();
   133   if (json.error_code !== 0) {
   134     throw new Error(`OCR API error ${json.error_code}: ${json.error_msg}`);
   135   }
   136 
   137   return json;
   138 }
   139 
   140 // 使用示例
   141 import { readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
   142 
   143 const imageBuffer = await readFile("./sample.jpg");
   144 const imageBase64 = btoa(String.fromCharCode(...new Uint8Array(imageBuffer)));
   145 const result = await callOcrAccurateBasic(imageBase64, {
   146   languageType: "CHN_ENG",
   147   detectDirection: true,
   148   probability: true,
   149 });
   150 
   151 if (result.words_result && result.words_result.length > 0) {
   152   console.log(`识别到 ${result.words_result.length} 行文字：`);
   153   result.words_result.forEach((item, idx) => {
   154     console.log(`[${idx + 1}] ${item.words}`);
   155   });
   156 } else {
   157   console.log("未识别到文字内容");
   158 }
   159 ```

   162 
   163 ## Edge Function 代码
   164 
   165 ### Web 平台（及通用版本）
   166 
   167 适用于 Web 和 MiniProgram 平台——两平台响应均为 JSON，实现相同。
   168 
   169 ```typescript
   170 // edge-functions/accurate-ocr.ts
   171 import { serve } from "https://deno.land/std/http/server.ts";
   172 
   173 serve(async (req: Request): Promise<Response> => {
   174   if (req.method !== "POST") {
   175     return new Response("Method Not Allowed", { status: 405 });
   176   }
   177 
   178   // --- 解析客户端请求 ---
   179   let image: string;
   180   let languageType: string | undefined;
   181   let detectDirection: boolean | undefined;
   182   let probability: boolean | undefined;
   183   let multidirectionalRecognize: boolean | undefined;
   184   let ofdFileNum: string | undefined;
   185 
   186   try {
   187     const body = await req.json();
   188     image = body.image;
   189     if (!image) throw new Error("Missing image");
   190     languageType = body.language_type;
   191     detectDirection = body.detect_direction;
   192     probability = body.probability;
   193     multidirectionalRecognize = body.multidirectional_recognize;
   194     ofdFileNum = body.ofd_file_num;
   195   } catch {
   196     return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: "Invalid request body" }), {
   197       status: 400,
   198       headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
   199     });
   200   }
   201 
   202   // --- 注入平台密钥（禁止暴露给客户端） ---
   203   const apiKey = Deno.env.get("INTEGRATIONS_API_KEY");
   204   if (!apiKey) {
   205     return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: "Server configuration error" }), {
   206       status: 500,
   207       headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
   208     });
   209   }
   210 
   211   // --- 构造上游请求参数 ---
   212   const params: Record<string, string> = { image };
   213   if (languageType) params.language_type = languageType;
   214   if (detectDirection !== undefined) params.detect_direction = String(detectDirection);
   215   if (probability !== undefined) params.probability = String(probability);
   216   if (multidirectionalRecognize !== undefined) {
   217     params.multidirectional_recognize = String(multidirectionalRecognize);
   218   }
   219   if (ofdFileNum) params.ofd_file_num = ofdFileNum;
   220 
   221   // --- 调用上游 ---
   222   const upstream = await fetch(
   223     "https://app-cui721lyjt35-api-eLMlJ2jB44g9-gateway.appmiaoda.com/rest/2.0/ocr/v1/accurate_basic",
   224     {
   225       method: "POST",
   226       headers: {
   227         "Content-Type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded",
   228         "X-Gateway-Authorization": `Bearer ${apiKey}`,
   229       },
   230       body: new URLSearchParams(params).toString(),
   231     }
   232   );
   233 
   234   // 透传配额/余额错误
   235   if (upstream.status === 429 || upstream.status === 402) {
   236     const errText = await upstream.text();
   237     return new Response(errText, {
   238       status: upstream.status,
   239       headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
   240     });
   241   }
   242 
   243   if (!upstream.ok) {
   244     return new Response(
   245       JSON.stringify({ error: `Upstream error: ${upstream.status}` }),
   246       { status: 502, headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" } }
   247     );
   248   }
   249 
   250   const data = await upstream.json();
   251   return new Response(JSON.stringify(data), {
   252     status: 200,
   253     headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
   254   });
   255 });
   256 ```
   257 
   258 ---
   259 
   260 ## 前端调用代码
   261 
   262 ### Web 平台
   263 
   264 **推荐方式（supabase client 可用时）：**
   265 
   266 ```typescript
   267 import { createClient } from "@supabase/supabase-js";
   268 
   269 const supabase = createClient(
   270   import.meta.env.VITE_SUPABASE_URL,
   271   import.meta.env.VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY
   272 );
   273 
   274 interface OcrWord {
   275   words: string;
   276   probability?: { average: number; variance: number; min: number };
   277 }
   278 
   279 interface OcrResult {
   280   log_id: number;
   281   direction?: number;
   282   words_result_num: number;
   283   words_result: OcrWord[];
   284   error_code: number;
   285   error_msg: string;
   286 }
   287 
   288 /**
   289  * 通过 Edge Function 调用通用文字识别（高精度版）。
   290  * @param imageBase64 - 图片的 Base64 编码字符串
   291  * @param languageType - 识别语言类型，默认 CHN_ENG
   292  * @returns OcrResult 识别结果
   293  */
   294 async function fetchOcrAccurateBasic(
   295   imageBase64: string,
   296   languageType = "CHN_ENG"
   297 ): Promise<OcrResult> {
   298   const { data, error } = await supabase.functions.invoke("accurate-ocr", {
   299     body: { image: imageBase64, language_type: languageType },
   300   });
   301   if (error) throw error;
   302   if (data.error_code !== 0) {
   303     throw new Error(`OCR API error ${data.error_code}: ${data.error_msg}`);
   304   }
   305   return data as OcrResult;
   306 }
   307 
   308 // 用法示例：从 <input type="file"> 读取图片并识别
   309 async function handleFileInput(file: File): Promise<void> {
   310   const buffer = await file.arrayBuffer();
   311   const bytes = new Uint8Array(buffer);
   312   let binary = "";
   313   const chunkSize = 8192;
   314   for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i += chunkSize) {
   315     binary += String.fromCharCode(...bytes.subarray(i, i + chunkSize));
   316   }
   317   const imageBase64 = btoa(binary);
   318 
   319   const result = await fetchOcrAccurateBasic(imageBase64);
   320   console.log("识别结果：", result.words_result.map((w) => w.words).join("\n"));
   321 }
   322 ```
   323 
   324 **备用方式（无法使用 supabase client 时）：**
   325 
   326 ```typescript
   327 /**
   328  * 通过原生 fetch 调用通用文字识别 Edge Function。
   329  * @param imageBase64 - 图片的 Base64 编码字符串
   330  * @param languageType - 识别语言类型，默认 CHN_ENG
   331  * @returns OcrResult 识别结果
   332  */
   333 async function fetchOcrAccurateBasicRaw(
   334   imageBase64: string,
   335   languageType = "CHN_ENG"
   336 ): Promise<OcrResult> {
   337   const res = await fetch(
   338     `${import.meta.env.VITE_SUPABASE_URL}/functions/v1/accurate-ocr`,
   339     {
   340       method: "POST",
   341       headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
   342       body: JSON.stringify({ image: imageBase64, language_type: languageType }),
   343     }
   344   );
   345 
   346   if (res.status === 429) {
   347     const err = await res.json();
   348     throw new Error(`配额已用尽：${err.message ?? res.statusText}`);
   349   }
   350   if (res.status === 402) {
   351     const err = await res.json();
   352     throw new Error(`余额不足：${err.message ?? res.statusText}`);
   353   }
   354   if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`请求失败：${res.status}`);
   355 
   356   const json = await res.json();
   357   if (json.error_code !== 0) {
   358     throw new Error(`OCR API error ${json.error_code}: ${json.error_msg}`);
   359   }
   360   return json as OcrResult;
   361 }
   362 ```
   363 
   364 ### MiniProgram 平台（Taro）
   365 
   366 ```typescript
   367 import Taro from "@tarojs/taro";
   368 import { createClient } from "@supabase/supabase-js";
   369 
   370 const supabase = createClient(
   371   process.env.TARO_APP_SUPABASE_URL!,
   372   process.env.TARO_APP_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY!
   373 );
   374 
   375 /**
   376  * MiniProgram 中将本地图片文件转为 Base64 字符串。
   377  * @param filePath - 本地临时文件路径（如相机或相册选择的图片）
   378  * @returns Base64 编码字符串
   379  */
   380 async function fileToBase64(filePath: string): Promise<string> {
   381   return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
   382     const fs = Taro.getFileSystemManager();
   383     fs.readFile({
   384       filePath,
   385       encoding: "base64",
   386       success: (res) => resolve(res.data as string),
   387       fail: (err) => reject(new Error(JSON.stringify(err))),
   388     });
   389   });
   390 }
   391 
   392 /**
   393  * MiniProgram 中通过 Edge Function 调用通用文字识别（高精度版）。
   394  * @param imageBase64 - 图片的 Base64 编码字符串
   395  * @param languageType - 识别语言类型，默认 CHN_ENG
   396  * @returns OcrResult 识别结果
   397  */
   398 async function fetchOcrMiniProgram(
   399   imageBase64: string,
   400   languageType = "CHN_ENG"
   401 ): Promise<OcrResult> {
   402   const { data, error } = await supabase.functions.invoke("accurate-ocr", {
   403     body: { image: imageBase64, language_type: languageType },
   404   });
   405   if (error) throw error;
   406   if (data.error_code !== 0) {
   407     throw new Error(`OCR API error ${data.error_code}: ${data.error_msg}`);
   408   }
   409   return data as OcrResult;
   410 }
   411 
   412 // 用法示例：从相册选取图片并识别
   413 const handleChooseImage = async () => {
   414   const res = await Taro.chooseImage({ count: 1, sourceType: ["album", "camera"] });
   415   const filePath = res.tempFilePaths[0];
   416   const imageBase64 = await fileToBase64(filePath);
   417   const result = await fetchOcrMiniProgram(imageBase64);
   418   console.log("识别结果：", result.words_result.map((w) => w.words).join("\n"));
   419 };
   420 ```
   421 
   422 ---
   423 
   424 ## 注意事项
   425 
   426 1. **密钥安全**：`INTEGRATIONS_API_KEY` 仅可在 Edge Function 服务端读取，严禁暴露到前端或客户端代码。
   427 
   428 2. **计费**：
   429    - 折后单价：**¥0.25 / 次**（原价 ¥0.30 / 次）
   430    - 每次调用均计费，避免在循环或高频场景中不必要地重复调用。
   431 
   432 3. **错误处理**：务必处理以下情况：
   433    - HTTP 429（配额超限）：已在 Edge Function 中透传，前端需提示用户稍后重试。
   434    - HTTP 402（余额不足）：已在 Edge Function 中透传，前端需提示充值。
   435    - `error_code !== 0`：OCR 服务自身错误，参考 `error_msg` 排查。
   436 
   437 4. **图片格式**：仅支持 jpg、jpeg、png、bmp 格式，上传前需客户端校验。
   438 
   439 5. **Base64 编码**：对于大图片，使用分块编码（chunkSize=8192）避免 `RangeError: Maximum call stack size exceeded`，禁止使用 `String.fromCharCode(...largeArray)` 展开写法。
   440 
   441 6. **MiniProgram 注意**：微信小程序中 `Taro.getFileSystemManager().readFile` 的 `encoding: "base64"` 返回的已是纯 Base64 字符串，无需再做 btoa 处理。
   442 
   443 7. **响应有效性判断**：不要用 `words_result_num > 0` 来判断是否有识别结果，应改为检查 `words_result` 数组本身（如 `words_result && words_result.length > 0`）。原因是当仅有段落结果（`paragraphs_result` 有值）而无逐行文字结果时，`words_result_num` 可能为 0，但 `words_result` 数组实际上可能并不为空。
   444 


</observation>

Covers Supabase server-side Schema, RLS, Edge Functions, Storage, Realtime, and Auth configuration.\n\n## Initial Setup\n\n- Use `supabase_init` to initialize the database or start it from a paused state\n\n## Schema Design\n\n- Minimize tables and columns; do not rely on migration files\n- Under no circumstances should you view or modify migration files\n- If frontend requires a feature without a corresponding table, policy, or edge function, you may add it afterward\n- When modifying business data (non-DDL, policy, RPC, etc.), use `supabase_execute_sql`\n- Before writing api.ts, read the corresponding `supabase/migrations/*.sql` to confirm NOT NULL constraints for every column — context compression may lose field details; migrations are the ground truth\n\n## Security Policies (RLS)\n\n### Design Principles\n\n- **Every table MUST have RLS enabled** — no exceptions, regardless of whether login auth exists\n- **Full coverage per table**: Explicitly define policies for every combination of SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE × {anon, authenticated, admin}. **Never leave any blank** — missing policies silently return empty data or 0 affected rows\n  - Per-table review checklist: anon × {S/I/U/D}, authenticated × {S/I/U/D}, admin × {S/I/U/D}\n  - Anticipate future features: add corresponding constraints now for role×operation combinations that may be needed later\n- **INSERT always requires a matching SELECT**: Any role granted INSERT permission MUST also have a SELECT policy defined\n  - Example: If a policy "allows anyone to submit", add one so "submitters can view their own submissions"\n- Frontend and backend permissions must be aligned\n  - No permission, no interaction\n  - All submit-capable forms must have explicit, role-based permissions\n- PostgREST silently returns 0 affected rows (no error) when UPDATE/DELETE does not match RLS — writes appear to succeed but nothing changed. Tables the frontend needs to mutate must have explicit UPDATE/DELETE policies defined\n- Clear role boundaries\n  - Admins must have full read/write access\n  - Roles must have complete and secure permissions defined for every possible interaction scenario\n- All internal checks must use `SECURITY DEFINER`\n\n### Implementation Notes\n\n- RLS should only depend on **static columns or secure functions**\n- **Policy self-loop**: A policy\'s USING clause must not query the same table it protects — use a `SECURITY DEFINER` helper function instead (it bypasses RLS when querying the table)\n- Use public views to expose read-only access to selected fields of high-privilege tables\n- **The column checked by `auth.uid()` MUST auto-fill**: For each INSERT policy whose WITH CHECK contains `<col> = auth.uid()`, that exact `<col>` MUST be declared `NOT NULL DEFAULT auth.uid()`. The client never sends it. Apply this to that one column only — never blanket-default other uuid/FK columns (e.g. `assigned_to`), which would be a bug.\n  - ❌ `owner_id uuid NOT NULL` + client `.insert(payload)` → owner is NULL → "new row violates row-level security policy"\n  - ✅ `owner_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT auth.uid()`\n\n## Storage Buckets\n\n- Create for image/file uploads only\n- **CRITICAL**: NEVER store images/videos as Base64 in database\n- ALL image/video assets MUST be uploaded to Supabase Storage\n- When third-party APIs return image/video URLs, transfer to Storage in Edge Function\n- buckets must be created in migration SQL via `supabase_apply_migration` — **do not** create them dynamically inside Edge Functions\n- Frontend validation: 1MB limit, snake_case filenames\n\n\n## Edge Functions\n\n### When to Use\n\n- **MANDATORY for third-party API calls**: ALL external API integrations (payment, weather, maps, etc.) MUST be implemented through Edge Functions\n- **STRICTLY FORBIDDEN**: Direct third-party API calls from client-side code (fetch/axios in frontend components)\n- Server-side secrets and service-role operations\n- Sensitive payment logic requiring secure environment\n\n### Authoring\n\n- Create files under `supabase/functions/<function_name>/`\n- **CRITICAL**: Use `supabase_deploy_edge_function` to deploy after modifying or creating Edge Function files\n- Put cross-domain content in the `_shared/` directory\n- No URL imports — only npm and JSR\n- Do not add secrets unless explicitly required by API documentation\n- Handle CORS properly\n- `supabase.sql` forbidden in Edge Functions\n- `supabase.raw` forbidden in Edge Functions — use `.rpc()` for atomic operations\n- Response Content-Type must be chosen only from: `[\'application/json\', \'application/octet-stream\', \'text/event-stream\', \'multipart/form-data\']`\n- Make proper use of optimistic locking to ensure that subsequent settlement operations in the state flow are executed only once, even if called multiple times\n\n\n## Supabase Auth\n\n- Your knowledge may be outdated. **MUST** call `skill_action(skill="login")` to refresh with the latest information\n- Any form of third-party login (OAuth or SSO) must use the login skill as the knowledge basis\n\n## Supabase Realtime (low-frequency feature)\n\nWhen realtime capability is genuinely needed, read `references/realtime.md` — it covers server-side publication switches, per-stack client subscription patterns, and RN/weapp limitations.\n\n**Multi-identity scenarios**: Identity selection / role assignment logic **must NOT** be placed on the frontend — it **MUST** be encapsulated in an Edge Function.', observation=<ObservationType.SKILL: 'skill'>, skill='supabase-server', success=True,      6 - Access: `import.meta.env.VITE_SUPABASE_URL` / `import.meta.env.VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY`
     7 - Written automatically into `.env` after `supabase_init`
     8 
     9 ## Type Definitions
    10 
    11 - Define types in `@/types/types.ts` matching SQL schema
    12 - When unsure whether a field exists, query the table structure first
    13 
    14 ## api.ts Coding Standards
    15 
    16 - Use `.maybeSingle()` instead of `.single()`
    17 - Always use `.order()` with `.limit()`
    18 - When writing select queries, avoid `table_name(*)` — use `table_name!foreign_key_name` to explicitly specify relationships and prevent ambiguity
    19 - Implement pagination for multiple results — **strictly prohibit** fetching all data without pagination
    20 - **Prefer cursor-based pagination**
    21 - Return arrays safely: `Array.isArray(data) ? data : []`
    22 - Empty strings need to be converted to NULL to prevent SQL formatting misalignment
    23 - Prefer `.insert()` without `.select()`
    24 - Protect nulls: `meeting.participants?.length`, `meeting.title || 'Untitled'`
    25 - Use Supabase Realtime for real-time data updates instead of polling
    26 
    27 ## Storage Upload (Frontend)
    28 
    29 - Create for image/file uploads only
    30 - **NEVER** store images/videos as Base64 in database
    31 - Frontend validation: 1MB limit, snake_case filenames
    32 - Web: upload directly using `File` / `Blob`:
    33 
    34 ```ts
    35 const { data, error } = await supabase.storage
    36   .from(bucket)
    37   .upload(`${userId}/${snakeCaseName}.jpg`, file, { contentType: file.type });
    38 
    39 const { data: urlData } = supabase.storage.from(bucket).getPublicUrl(data.path);
    40 // Store urlData.publicUrl in database
    41 ```
    42 
    43 ## Edge Function Invocation
    44 
    45 - Always use `supabase.functions.invoke`, except when a non-standard or custom Content-Type is required
    46 - Read `error.context.text()` for the real error message
    47 - For GET requests with parameters, append query parameters directly to the function name: `supabase.functions.invoke('test-fn?id=123', { method: 'GET' })`
    48 
    49 ```ts
    50 // Type signature
    51 supabase.functions.invoke<T = any>(
    52   functionName: string,
    53   options?: {
    54     body?: any
    55     headers?: Record<string, string>
    56     method?: 'GET' | 'POST' | 'PUT' | 'PATCH' | 'DELETE'
    57     signal?: AbortSignal
    58   }
    59 ): Promise<{
    60   data: T | null
    61   error: FunctionsHttpError | null // Read error.context.text() for real message
    62 }>
    63 
    64 const { data, error } = await supabase.functions.invoke('hello_world', {
    65   body: { key: 'value' },
    66   method: 'POST',
    67 });
    68 
    69 if (error) {
    70   const errorMsg = await error?.context?.text();
    71   console.error("edge function error in <hello_world>:", errorMsg || error?.message);
    72 }
    73 ```
    74 
    75 ## Realtime Subscription (low-frequency feature)
    76 
    77 Use only when realtime capability is genuinely needed. Full spec (server-side publication switch, subscription template, per-stack differences) is in `supabase-server/references/realtime.md`.
    78 
    79 ## Auth
    80 
    81 For login/signup/OAuth/SSO implementation, follow the `login` skill — **MUST** call `skill_action(skill="login")` to get the latest spec.
    82

```
import { serve } from "https://deno.land/std@0.168.0/http/server.ts";

interface OcrWord {
  words: string;
  probability?: { average: number; variance: number; min: number };
}

interface OcrResult {
  log_id: number;
  direction?: number;
  words_result_num: number;
  words_result: OcrWord[];
  paragraphs_result?: Array<{ words_result_idx: number[] }>;
  paragraphs_result_num?: number;
  error_code: number;
  error_msg: string;
}

serve(async (req: Request): Promise<Response> => {
  if (req.method !== "POST") {
    return new Response("Method Not Allowed", { status: 405 });
  }

  let image: string;
  let languageType: string | undefined;
  try {
    const body = await req.json();
    image = body.image;
    if (!image) throw new Error("Missing image");
    languageType = body.language_type;
  } catch {
    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: "Invalid request body" }), {
      status: 400,
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    });
  }

  const apiKey = Deno.env.get("INTEGRATIONS_API_KEY");
  if (!apiKey) {
    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: "Server configuration error" }), {
      status: 500,
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    });
  }

  const params: Record<string, string> = { image };
  if (languageType) params.language_type = languageType;

  const upstream = await fetch(
    "https://app-cui721lyjt35-api-eLMlJ2jB44g9-gateway.appmiaoda.com/rest/2.0/ocr/v1/accurate_basic",
    {
      method: "POST",
      headers: {
        "Content-Type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded",
        "X-Gateway-Authorization": `Bearer ${apiKey}`,
      },
      body: new URLSearchParams(params).toString(),
    }
  );

  if (upstream.status === 429 || upstream.status === 402) {
    const errText = await upstream.text();
    return new Response(errText, {
      status: upstream.status,
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    });
  }

  if (!upstream.ok) {
    return new Response(
      JSON.stringify({ error: `Upstream error: ${upstream.status}` }),
      { status: 502, headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" } }
    );
  }

  const data = (await upstream.json()) as OcrResult;
  return new Response(JSON.stringify(data), {
    status: 200,
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
  });
});

```

**FileEditAction**
Path: [/workspace/app-cui721lyjt35/supabase/functions/word-info/index.ts]
Thought: 
Command: create
Created File with Text:
```
import { serve } from "https://deno.land/std@0.168.0/http/server.ts";

interface WordResult {
  word: string;
  phonetic: string;
  translation: string;
}

interface TranslateResult {
  from: string;
  to: string;
  trans_result: Array<{ src: string; dst: string }>;
}

interface DictionaryEntry {
  word: string;
  phonetic?: string;
  phonetics?: Array<{ text?: string; audio?: string }>;
}

const TRANSLATION_API =
  "https://app-cui721lyjt35-api-e94GZ5j0PWpa-gateway.appmiaoda.com/rpc/2.0/mt/texttrans/v1";

function normalizeWord(input: string): string {
  return input
    .replace(/[^a-zA-Z'-]/g, "")
    .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, "")
    .toLowerCase();
}

async function withConcurrencyLimit<T, R>(
  items: T[],
  limit: number,
  fn: (item: T) => Promise<R>
): Promise<R[]> {
  const results: R[] = new Array(items.length);
  let index = 0;

  async function worker() {
    while (index < items.length) {
      const currentIndex = index++;
      results[currentIndex] = await fn(items[currentIndex]);
    }
  }

  const workers: Promise<void>[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(limit, items.length); i++) {
    workers.push(worker());
  }
  await Promise.all(workers);
  return results;
}

async function translateWords(
  words: string[],
  apiKey: string
): Promise<Record<string, string>> {
  const q = words.join("\n");
  const upstream = await fetch(TRANSLATION_API, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: {
      "Content-Type": "application/json;charset=utf-8",
      "X-Gateway-Authorization": `Bearer ${apiKey}`,
    },
    body: JSON.stringify({ q, from: "en", to: "zh" }),
  });

  if (upstream.status === 429 || upstream.status === 402) {
    const errText = await upstream.text();
    throw new Error(errText);
  }

  if (!upstream.ok) {
    throw new Error(`Translation upstream error: ${upstream.status}`);
  }

  const json = (await upstream.json()) as {
    error_code?: number;
    error_msg?: string;
    result?: TranslateResult;
  };

  if (json.error_code) {
    throw new Error(`Translation API error ${json.error_code}: ${json.error_msg}`);
  }

  const map: Record<string, string> = {};
  const transResult = json.result?.trans_result ?? [];
  for (let i = 0; i < words.length && i < transResult.length; i++) {
    map[words[i]] = transResult[i].dst;
  }
  return map;
}

async function fetchPhonetic(word: string): Promise<string> {
  try {
    const res = await fetch(
      `https://api.dictionaryapi.dev/api/v2/entries/en/${encodeURIComponent(word)}`,
      { method: "GET" }
    );
    if (!res.ok) return "";
    const entries = (await res.json()) as DictionaryEntry[];
    const entry = entries.find((e) => e.word.toLowerCase() === word.toLowerCase());
    if (!entry) return "";
    if (entry.phonetic) return entry.phonetic;
    for (const p of entry.phonetics ?? []) {
      if (p.text) return p.text;
    }
    return "";
  } catch {
    return "";
  }
}

serve(async (req: Request): Promise<Response> => {
  if (req.method !== "POST") {
    return new Response("Method Not Allowed", { status: 405 });
  }

  let rawWords: unknown;
  try {
    const body = await req.json();
    rawWords = body.words;
    if (!Array.isArray(rawWords) || rawWords.length === 0) {
      throw new Error("Missing or invalid words");
    }
  } catch {
    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: "Invalid request body" }), {
      status: 400,
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    });
  }

  const apiKey = Deno.env.get("INTEGRATIONS_API_KEY");
  if (!apiKey) {
    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: "Server configuration error" }), {
      status: 500,
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    });
  }

  const words = (rawWords as string[])
    .map((w) => normalizeWord(String(w)))
    .filter((w) => w.length > 0);

  if (words.length === 0) {
    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ result: [] }), {
      status: 200,
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    });
  }

  try {
    const translationMap = await translateWords(words, apiKey);
    const phonetics = await withConcurrencyLimit(words, 5, fetchPhonetic);

    const result: WordResult[] = words.map((word, i) => ({
      word,
      phonetic: phonetics[i] ?? "",
      translation: translationMap[word] ?? "",
    }));

    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ result }), {
      status: 200,
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    });
  } catch (err) {
    const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Unknown error";
    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: message }), {
      status: 502,
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    });
  }
});

```

</action>

Path: [/workspace/app-cui721lyjt35/src/utils/fileToBase64.ts]
Thought:

Created File with Text:
```
/**
 * 将 File 对象转换为纯 Base64 字符串（不含 data URL 前缀）。
 * 采用分块读取，避免大图片触发 RangeError。
 */
export async function fileToBase64(file: File): Promise<string> {
  const buffer = await file.arrayBuffer();
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(buffer);
  const chunkSize = 8192;
  let binary = "";
  for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i += chunkSize) {
    binary += String.fromCharCode(...bytes.subarray(i, i + chunkSize));
  }
  return btoa(binary);
}

/**
 * 验证图片文件类型与大小。
 * 仅接受 jpg/jpeg/png/bmp，单张不超过 5MB。
 */
export function validateImage(file: File): { valid: boolean; message?: string } {
  const allowedTypes = ["image/jpeg", "image/jpg", "image/png", "image/bmp"];
  if (!allowedTypes.includes(file.type)) {
    return { valid: false, message: `不支持的文件类型：${file.name}` };
  }
  const maxSize = 5 * 1024 * 1024;
  if (file.size > maxSize) {
    return { valid: false, message: `图片大小超过 5MB：${file.name}` };
  }
  return { valid: true };
}

```

```
import { supabase } from "@/db/supabase";

export interface OcrWord {
  words: string;
  probability?: { average: number; variance: number; min: number };
}

export interface OcrResult {
  log_id: number;
  direction?: number;
  words_result_num: number;
  words_result: OcrWord[];
  error_code: number;
  error_msg: string;
}

/**
 * 调用 Edge Function 进行高精度 OCR 识别。
 */
export async function recognizeImage(imageBase64: string): Promise<OcrResult> {
  const { data, error } = await supabase.functions.invoke<OcrResult>("accurate-ocr", {
    body: { image: imageBase64, language_type: "ENG" },
  });

  if (error) {
    const errorMsg = await error.context?.text();
    throw new Error(errorMsg || error.message || "OCR 识别失败");
  }

  if (!data) {
    throw new Error("OCR 未返回结果");
  }

  if (data.error_code !== 0) {
    throw new Error(`OCR 识别出错：${data.error_msg}`);
  }

  return data;
}

```

import { supabase } from "@/db/supabase";

export interface WordInfo {
  word: string;
  phonetic: string;
  translation: string;
}

export interface WordInfoResponse {
  result: WordInfo[];
}

/**
 * 批量查询单词的音标与中文翻译。
 */
export async function fetchWordInfo(words: string[]): Promise<WordInfo[]> {
  const { data, error } = await supabase.functions.invoke<WordInfoResponse>("word-info", {
    body: { words },
  });

  if (error) {
    const errorMsg = await error.context?.text();
    throw new Error(errorMsg || error.message || "单词查询失败");
  }

  if (!data) {
    throw new Error("单词查询未返回结果");
  }

  return data.result ?? [];
}

```
import type { WordInfo } from "@/services/wordInfoService";

/**
 * 将单词信息数组导出为 UTF-8 编码的 CSV 文件。
 */
export function exportWordInfoToCsv(words: WordInfo[]): void {
  if (words.length === 0) return;

  const header = ["单词", "音标", "中译"];
  const rows = words.map((item) => [
    escapeCsvField(item.word),
    escapeCsvField(item.phonetic),
    escapeCsvField(item.translation),
  ]);

  const csvContent = [header, ...rows].map((row) => row.join(",")).join("\n");
  const bom = "\uFEFF";
  const blob = new Blob([bom + csvContent], { type: "text/csv;charset=utf-8;" });

  const link = document.createElement("a");
  link.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  link.download = `单词识别结果_${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}.csv`;
  document.body.appendChild(link);
  link.click();
  document.body.removeChild(link);
  URL.revokeObjectURL(link.href);
}

function escapeCsvField(value: string): string {
  if (!value) return "";
  const escaped = value.replace(/"/g, '""');
  if (escaped.includes(",") || escaped.includes('"') || escaped.includes("\n")) {
    return `"${escaped}"`;
  }
  return escaped;
}

```

Path: [/workspace/app-cui721lyjt35/src/utils/extractWords.ts]
Thought: 
Command: create
Created File with Text:
```
/**
 * 从 OCR 识别出的原始文本中提取英文单词。
 * 按空格、换行、常见标点分割，过滤非字母字符并统一小写。
 */
export function extractEnglishWords(texts: string[]): string[] {
  const words: string[] = [];
  for (const text of texts) {
    const segments = text
      .replace(/[\r\n]+/g, " ")
      .replace(/[^a-zA-Z\s'-]/g, " ")
      .split(/\s+/);
    for (const segment of segments) {
      const cleaned = segment
        .replace(/^[^a-zA-Z]+|[^a-zA-Z]+$/g, "")
        .toLowerCase();
      if (cleaned.length > 0 && /^[a-zA-Z'-]+$/.test(cleaned)) {
        words.push(cleaned);
      }
    }
  }
  return words;
}

```

</action>

**MCPAction**
NAME: generate_success
ARGUMENTS: {'appId': 'app-cui721lyjt35', 'userId': 'user-ad1kbqs40lc0', 'prd_content': '# 需求文档\n\n## 1. 应用概述\n\n**应用名称**：单词图片识别工具\n\n**应用描述**：用户上传包含英语单词的图片，应用通过OCR识别图片中的单词，自动查询每个单词的音标和中文翻译，生成表格展示结果，支持导出表格。\n\n## 2. 页面结构与功能说明\n\n### 2.1 页面结构\n\n```\n单词图片识别工具\n└── 主页面\n    ├── 图片上传区域\n    ├── 识别结果展示区域\n    └── 导出功能区域\n```\n\n### 2.2 主页面功能说明\n\n#### 2.2.1 图片上传区域\n\n- 用户可点击上传按钮选择本地图片文件\n- 支持多张图片同时上传\n- 上传后显示图片缩略图预览\n- 用户可删除已上传的图片\n- 提供「开始识别」按钮触发OCR识别\n\n#### 2.2.2 识别结果展示区域\n\n- 以表格形式展示识别结果\n- 表格列包含：单词、音标、中译\n- 每行对应一个识别出的单词及其信息\n- 多张图片的识别结果合并展示在同一表格中\n\n#### 2.2.3 导出功能区域\n\n- 提供「导出表格」按钮\n- 点击后将当前表格内容导出为文件\n\n## 3. 业务规则与逻辑\n\n### 3.1 OCR识别规则\n\n- 对上传的每张图片执行OCR识别\n- 提取图片中的英语单词文本\n- 识别结果按单词进行分词处理\n\n### 3.2 单词查询规则\n\n- 对识别出的每个单词查询音标和中文翻译\n- 查询结果填充到表格对应列中\n\n### 3.3 结果合并规则\n\n- 多张图片的识别结果按上传顺序依次合并到表格中\n- 相同单词重复出现时，每次出现均作为独立行展示\n\n### 3.4 导出规则\n\n- 导出文件包含表格的全部内容\n- 导出格式为常见表格文件格式\n\n## 4. 异常与边界情况\n\n| 场景 | 处理方式 |\n|------|----------|\n| 用户未上传图片直接点击「开始识别」 | 提示用户需先上传图片 |\n| 图片中未识别到任何单词 | 提示「未识别到单词」 |\n| 单词查询失败（无音标或翻译） | 对应列显示为空或显示「查询失败」 |\n| 用户在无识别结果时点击「导出表格」 | 提示用户需先完成识别 |\n\n## 5. 验收标准\n\n1. 用户上传一张包含英语单词的图片\n2. 点击「开始识别」按钮\n3. 页面展示表格，包含识别出的单词、音标、中译\n4. 点击「导出表格」按钮\n5. 成功下载包含表格内容的文件\n\n## 6. 本期不实现功能\n\n- 单词去重功能\n- 单词排序功能\n- 识别历史记录\n- 单词收藏或标记功能\n- 批量编辑表格内容\n- 自定义导出格式选择\n- 图片预处理（裁剪、旋转、增强）\n- 识别进度显示\n- 多语言支持（仅支持英语单词识别）'}
</action>

Path: [/workspace/app-cui721lyjt35/src/utils/compressImage.ts]
Thought: 
Command: create
Created File with Text:
```
/**
 * 使用 Canvas 对图片进行等比压缩。
 * 最大边长限制为 1920，输出 JPEG 格式，质量 0.8。
 * 返回压缩后的 Blob。
 */
export function compressImage(file: File, maxSide = 1920, quality = 0.8): Promise<Blob> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const img = new Image();
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(file);

    img.onload = () => {
      URL.revokeObjectURL(url);

      let { width, height } = img;
      const scale = Math.min(1, maxSide / Math.max(width, height));
      width = Math.round(width * scale);
      height = Math.round(height * scale);

      const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
      canvas.width = width;
      canvas.height = height;
      const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
      if (!ctx) {
        reject(new Error("无法创建 canvas 上下文"));
        return;
      }

      ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, width, height);
      canvas.toBlob(
        (blob) => {
          if (blob) {
            resolve(blob);
          } else {
            reject(new Error("图片压缩失败"));
          }
        },
        "image/jpeg",
        quality
      );
    };

    img.onerror = () => {
      URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
      reject(new Error("图片加载失败"));
    };

    img.src = url;
  });
}

```

Thought:

Old String: ```
/**
 * 将 File 对象转换为纯 Base64 字符串（不含 data URL 前缀）。
 * 采用分块读取，避免大图片触发 RangeError。
 */
export async function fileToBase64(file: File): Promise<string> {
  const buffer = await file.arrayBuffer();
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(buffer);
  const chunkSize = 8192;
  let binary = "";
  for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i += chunkSize) {
    binary += String.fromCharCode(...bytes.subarray(i, i + chunkSize));
  }
  return btoa(binary);
}
```
New String: ```
import { compressImage } from "./compressImage";

/**
 * 将 File 对象转换为纯 Base64 字符串（不含 data URL 前缀）。
 * 先对图片进行等比压缩，再采用分块读取，避免大图片触发 RangeError 或请求体超限。
 */
export async function fileToBase64(file: File): Promise<string> {
  const compressed = await compressImage(file);
  const buffer = await compressed.arrayBuffer();
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(buffer);
  const chunkSize = 8192;
  let binary = "";
  for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i += chunkSize) {
    binary += String.fromCharCode(...bytes.subarray(i, i + chunkSize));
  }
  return btoa(binary);
}
```

    const allWords: string[] = [];
    let failedCount = 0;

    try {
      for (const item of files) {
        try {
          const base64 = await fileToBase64(item.file);
          const ocrResult = await recognizeImage(base64);
          const texts = ocrResult.words_result.map((w) => w.words);
          const words = extractEnglishWords(texts);
          allWords.push(...words);
        } catch (itemErr) {
          failedCount++;
          const itemMessage = itemErr instanceof Error ? itemErr.message : "未知错误";
          toast.error(`图片「${item.file.name}」识别失败：${itemMessage}`);
        }
      }

      if (allWords.length === 0) {
        if (failedCount > 0) {
          toast.error("所有图片均识别失败，请检查图片内容或网络后重试", { id: toastId });
        } else {
          toast.success("未识别到任何英文单词", { id: toastId });
        }
        return;
      }

      if (failedCount > 0) {
        toast.warning(`${failedCount} 张图片识别失败，已跳过`, { id: toastId });
      } else {
        toast.loading(`已识别 ${allWords.length} 个单词，正在查询释义…`, { id: toastId });
      }

      const wordInfos = await fetchWordInfo(allWords);

      setResults(wordInfos);
      toast.success(`成功识别并查询 ${wordInfos.length} 个单词`, { id: toastId });
    } catch (err) {
      const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "识别过程出现未知错误";
      toast.error(message, { id: toastId });
    } finally {
      setIsRecognizing(false);
    }

Old String: ```
/**
 * 验证图片文件类型与大小。
 * 仅接受 jpg/jpeg/png/bmp，单张不超过 5MB。
 */
export function validateImage(file: File): { valid: boolean; message?: string } {
  const allowedTypes = ["image/jpeg", "image/jpg", "image/png", "image/bmp"];
  if (!allowedTypes.includes(file.type)) {
    return { valid: false, message: `不支持的文件类型：${file.name}` };
  }
  const maxSize = 5 * 1024 * 1024;
  if (file.size > maxSize) {
    return { valid: false, message: `图片大小超过 5MB：${file.name}` };
  }
  return { valid: true };
}
```
New String: ```
/**
 * 验证图片文件类型与大小。
 * 接受 jpg/jpeg/png/bmp/webp，压缩时会统一转为 JPEG；单张不超过 10MB。
 */
export function validateImage(file: File): { valid: boolean; message?: string } {
  const allowedTypes = ["image/jpeg", "image/jpg", "image/png", "image/bmp", "image/webp"];
  if (!allowedTypes.includes(file.type)) {
    return { valid: false, message: `不支持的文件类型：${file.name}，请上传 JPG/PNG/BMP/WebP 图片` };
  }
  const maxSize = 10 * 1024 * 1024;
  if (file.size > maxSize) {
    return { valid: false, message: `图片大小超过 10MB：${file.name}` };
  }
  return { valid: true };
}

    24 function normalizeWord(input: string): string {

    26     .replace(/[^a-zA-Z'-]/g, "")
    27     .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, "")

    31 async function withConcurrencyLimit<T, R>(
    32   items: T[],
    33   limit: number,
    34   fn: (item: T) => Promise<R>
    35 ): Promise<R[]> {
    36   const results: R[] = new Array(items.length);
    37   let index = 0;
    38 
    39   async function worker() {
    40     while (index < items.length) {
    41       const currentIndex = index++;
    42       results[currentIndex] = await fn(items[currentIndex]);
    43     }
    44   }
    45 
    46   const workers: Promise<void>[] = [];
    47   for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(limit, items.length); i++) {
    48     workers.push(worker());
    49   }
    50   await Promise.all(workers);
    51   return results;
    52 }

    66   });
    67 
    68   if (upstream.status === 429 || upstream.status === 402) {
    69     const errText = await upstream.text();
    70     throw new Error(errText);
    71   }
    72 
    73   if (!upstream.ok) {
    74     throw new Error(`Translation upstream error: ${upstream.status}`);
    75   }
    76 
    77   const json = (await upstream.json()) as {
    78     error_code?: number;

    80     result?: TranslateResult;
    81   };
    82 
    83   if (json.error_code) {

    85   }
    86

    88   const transResult = json.result?.trans_result ?? [];
    89   for (let i = 0; i < words.length && i < transResult.length; i++) {
    90     map[words[i]] = transResult[i].dst;
    91   }
    92   return map;

    95 async function fetchPhonetic(word: string): Promise<string> {
    96   try {
    97     const res = await fetch(
    98       `https://api.dictionaryapi.dev/api/v2/entries/en/${encodeURIComponent(word)}`,
    99       { method: "GET" }
   100     );
   101     if (!res.ok) return "";
   102     const entries = (await res.json()) as DictionaryEntry[];
   103     const entry = entries.find((e) => e.word.toLowerCase() === word.toLowerCase());
   104     if (!entry) return "";
   105     if (entry.phonetic) return entry.phonetic;
   106     for (const p of entry.phonetics ?? []) {
   107       if (p.text) return p.text;
   108     }
   109     return "";
   110   } catch {
   111     return "";
   112   }
   113 }

import { serve } from "https://deno.land/std@0.168.0/http/server.ts";

interface OcrWord {
  words: string;
  probability?: { average: number; variance: number; min: number };
}

interface OcrResult {
  log_id: number;
  direction?: number;
  words_result_num: number;
  words_result: OcrWord[];
  paragraphs_result?: Array<{ words_result_idx: number[] }>;
  paragraphs_result_num?: number;
  error_code: number;
  error_msg: string;
}

serve(async (req: Request): Promise<Response> => {
  if (req.method !== "POST") {
    return new Response("Method Not Allowed", { status: 405 });
  }

  let image: string;
  let languageType: string | undefined;
  try {
    const body = await req.json();
    image = body.image;
    if (!image) throw new Error("Missing image");
    languageType = body.language_type;
  } catch {
    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: "Invalid request body" }), {
      status: 400,
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    });
  }

  const apiKey = Deno.env.get("INTEGRATIONS_API_KEY");
  if (!apiKey) {
    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: "Server configuration error" }), {
      status: 500,
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    });
  }

  const params: Record<string, string> = { image };
  if (languageType) params.language_type = languageType;

  const upstream = await fetch(
    "https://app-cui721lyjt35-api-eLMlJ2jB44g9-gateway.appmiaoda.com/rest/2.0/ocr/v1/accurate_basic",
    {
      method: "POST",
      headers: {
        "Content-Type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded",
        "X-Gateway-Authorization": `Bearer ${apiKey}`,
      },
      body: new URLSearchParams(params).toString(),
    }
  );

  if (upstream.status === 429 || upstream.status === 402) {
    const errText = await upstream.text();
    return new Response(errText, {
      status: upstream.status,
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    });
  }

  if (!upstream.ok) {
    return new Response(
      JSON.stringify({ error: `Upstream error: ${upstream.status}` }),
      { status: 502, headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" } }
    );
  }

  const data = (await upstream.json()) as OcrResult;
  return new Response(JSON.stringify(data), {
    status: 200,
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
  });
});

Old String: ```
/**
 * 使用 Canvas 对图片进行等比压缩。
 * 最大边长限制为 1920，输出 JPEG 格式，质量 0.8。
 * 返回压缩后的 Blob。
 */
export function compressImage(file: File, maxSide = 1920, quality = 0.8): Promise<Blob> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const img = new Image();
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(file);

    img.onload = () => {
      URL.revokeObjectURL(url);

      let { width, height } = img;
      const scale = Math.min(1, maxSide / Math.max(width, height));
      width = Math.round(width * scale);
      height = Math.round(height * scale);

      const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
      canvas.width = width;
      canvas.height = height;
      const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
      if (!ctx) {
        reject(new Error("无法创建 canvas 上下文"));
        return;
      }

      ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, width, height);
      canvas.toBlob(
        (blob) => {
          if (blob) {
            resolve(blob);
          } else {
            reject(new Error("图片压缩失败"));
          }
        },
        "image/jpeg",
        quality
      );
    };

    img.onerror = () => {
      URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
      reject(new Error("图片加载失败"));
    };

    img.src = url;
  });
}
```
New String: ```
/**
 * 使用 Canvas 对图片进行等比压缩。
 * 最大边长限制为 1920，输出 JPEG 格式，质量 0.8。
 * 返回压缩后的 Blob。
 */
export function compressImage(file: File, maxSide = 1920, quality = 0.8): Promise<Blob> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const img = new Image();
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(file);

    img.onload = () => {
      URL.revokeObjectURL(url);

      let { width, height } = img;
      if (!width || !height) {
        reject(new Error("图片尺寸异常，请更换图片格式后重试"));
        return;
      }

      const scale = Math.min(1, maxSide / Math.max(width, height));
      width = Math.round(width * scale);
      height = Math.round(height * scale);

      const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
      canvas.width = width;
      canvas.height = height;
      const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
      if (!ctx) {
        reject(new Error("浏览器不支持 Canvas 压缩，请尝试更换浏览器"));
        return;
      }

      ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, width, height);
      canvas.toBlob(
        (blob) => {
          if (blob && blob.size > 0) {
            resolve(blob);
          } else {
            reject(new Error("图片压缩失败，请尝试上传其他图片"));
          }
        },
        "image/jpeg",
        quality
      );
    };

    img.onerror = () => {
      URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
      reject(new Error("图片加载失败，请确认上传的是 JPG/PNG/BMP/WebP 格式"));
    };

    img.src = url;
  });
}
```

import { compressImage } from "./compressImage";

/**
 * 将 File 对象转换为纯 Base64 字符串（不含 data URL 前缀）。
 * 先对图片进行等比压缩，再采用分块读取，避免大图片触发 RangeError 或请求体超限。
 */
export async function fileToBase64(file: File): Promise<string> {
  const compressed = await compressImage(file);
  const buffer = await compressed.arrayBuffer();
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(buffer);
  const chunkSize = 8192;
  let binary = "";
  for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i += chunkSize) {
    binary += String.fromCharCode(...bytes.subarray(i, i + chunkSize));
  }
  return btoa(binary);
}
```
New String: ```
import { compressImage } from "./compressImage";

/**
 * 将 File 对象转换为纯 Base64 字符串（不含 data URL 前缀）。
 * 先尝试对图片进行等比压缩以减小请求体积；若压缩失败则回退到原始文件。
 * 采用分块读取，避免大图片触发 RangeError 或请求体超限。
 */
export async function fileToBase64(file: File): Promise<string> {
  let target: File | Blob = file;
  try {
    target = await compressImage(file);
  } catch (compressErr) {
    console.warn("图片压缩失败，回退到原始文件", compressErr);
  }

  const buffer = await target.arrayBuffer();
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(buffer);
  const chunkSize = 8192;
  let binary = "";
  for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i += chunkSize) {
    binary += String.fromCharCode(...bytes.subarray(i, i + chunkSize));
  }
  return btoa(binary);
}
```

  const onDrop = useCallback((acceptedFiles: File[]) => {
    const newFiles: PreviewFile[] = [];
    for (const file of acceptedFiles) {
      const validation = validateImage(file);
      if (!validation.valid) {
        toast.error(validation.message);
        continue;
      }
      newFiles.push({
        id: `${file.name}-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`,
        file,
        preview: URL.createObjectURL(file),
      });
    }
    if (newFiles.length > 0) {
      setFiles((prev) => [...prev, ...newFiles]);
    }
  }, []);

  const onDropRejected = useCallback((fileRejections: FileRejection[]) => {
    for (const rejection of fileRejections) {
      const errors = rejection.errors.map((e) => e.message).join("；");
      toast.error(`文件「${rejection.file.name}」被拒绝：${errors}`);
    }
  }, []);

  const { getRootProps, getInputProps, isDragActive, fileRejections } = useDropzone({
    onDrop,
    onDropRejected,
    accept: {
      "image/*": [".jpeg", ".jpg", ".png", ".bmp", ".webp"],
    },
    multiple: true,
  });

**FileEditAction**
Path: [/workspace/app-cui721lyjt35/src/pages/WordOcrPage.tsx]
Thought: 
Command: create
Created File with Text:
```
import React, { useCallback, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { useDropzone } from "react-dropzone";
import { FileImage, Loader2, Trash2, Upload, FileDown, ScanLine } from "lucide-react";
import { toast } from "sonner";

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Card, CardContent } from "@/components/ui/card";
import {
  Table,
  TableBody,
  TableCell,
  TableHead,
  TableHeader,
  TableRow,
} from "@/components/ui/table";

import { fileToBase64, validateImage } from "@/utils/fileToBase64";
import { extractEnglishWords } from "@/utils/extractWords";
import { exportWordInfoToCsv } from "@/utils/exportCsv";
import { recognizeImage } from "@/services/ocrService";
import { fetchWordInfo, type WordInfo } from "@/services/wordInfoService";

interface PreviewFile {
  id: string;
  file: File;
  preview: string;
}

export const WordOcrPage: React.FC = () => {
  const [files, setFiles] = useState<PreviewFile[]>([]);
  const [isRecognizing, setIsRecognizing] = useState(false);
  const [results, setResults] = useState<WordInfo[]>([]);

  const onDrop = useCallback((acceptedFiles: File[]) => {
    const newFiles: PreviewFile[] = [];
    for (const file of acceptedFiles) {
      const validation = validateImage(file);
      if (!validation.valid) {
        toast.error(validation.message);
        continue;
      }
      newFiles.push({
        id: `${file.name}-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`,
        file,
        preview: URL.createObjectURL(file),
      });
    }
    setFiles((prev) => [...prev, ...newFiles]);
  }, []);

  const { getRootProps, getInputProps, isDragActive } = useDropzone({
    onDrop,
    accept: {
      "image/*": [".jpeg", ".jpg", ".png", ".bmp"],
    },
    multiple: true,
  });

  const removeFile = useCallback((id: string) => {
    setFiles((prev) => {
      const target = prev.find((f) => f.id === id);
      if (target) URL.revokeObjectURL(target.preview);
      return prev.filter((f) => f.id !== id);
    });
  }, []);

  const handleRecognize = useCallback(async () => {
    if (files.length === 0) {
      toast.error("请先上传至少一张图片");
      return;
    }

    setIsRecognizing(true);
    setResults([]);
    const toastId = toast.loading("正在识别图片中的单词，请稍候…");

    try {
      const allWords: string[] = [];

      for (const item of files) {
        const base64 = await fileToBase64(item.file);
        const ocrResult = await recognizeImage(base64);
        const texts = ocrResult.words_result.map((w) => w.words);
        const words = extractEnglishWords(texts);
        allWords.push(...words);
      }

      if (allWords.length === 0) {
        toast.success("未识别到任何英文单词", { id: toastId });
        return;
      }

      toast.loading(`已识别 ${allWords.length} 个单词，正在查询释义…`, { id: toastId });
      const wordInfos = await fetchWordInfo(allWords);

      setResults(wordInfos);
      toast.success(`成功识别并查询 ${wordInfos.length} 个单词`, { id: toastId });
    } catch (err) {
      const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "识别过程出现未知错误";
      toast.error(message, { id: toastId });
    } finally {
      setIsRecognizing(false);
    }
  }, [files]);

  const handleExport = useCallback(() => {
    if (results.length === 0) {
      toast.error("暂无可导出的识别结果");
      return;
    }
    exportWordInfoToCsv(results);
    toast.success("表格已导出");
  }, [results]);

  const hasResults = results.length > 0;

  const tableRows = useMemo(
    () =>
      results.map((item, index) => (
        <TableRow key={`${item.word}-${index}`}>
          <TableCell className="font-medium">{item.word}</TableCell>
          <TableCell className="text-muted-foreground font-mono">{item.phonetic || "-"}</TableCell>
          <TableCell>{item.translation || "-"}</TableCell>
        </TableRow>
      )),
    [results]
  );

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen w-full bg-background text-foreground">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-5xl px-4 py-8 md:py-12">
        <header className="mb-8 md:mb-12">
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight md:text-3xl text-balance">
            单词图片识别工具
          </h1>
          <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground md:text-base max-w-prose">
            上传包含英语单词的图片，自动识别内容并生成「单词 / 音标 / 中译」表格。
          </p>
        </header>

        <Card className="border border-border bg-card">
          <CardContent className="p-4 md:p-6">
            <div
              {...getRootProps()}
              className={`
                cursor-pointer rounded-lg border border-dashed border-border bg-secondary/30
                px-4 py-8 text-center transition-colors hover:bg-secondary/60 md:py-12
                ${isDragActive ? "border-primary bg-primary/5" : ""}
              `}
            >
              <input {...getInputProps()} />
              <div className="mx-auto flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-muted">
                <Upload className="h-5 w-5 text-muted-foreground" />
              </div>
              <p className="mt-4 text-sm font-medium">拖拽图片到此处，或点击选择图片</p>
              <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">支持 JPG、PNG、BMP，单张不超过 5MB</p>
            </div>

            {files.length > 0 && (
              <div className="mt-6 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3 md:grid-cols-4">
                {files.map((item) => (
                  <div
                    key={item.id}
                    className="group relative aspect-square overflow-hidden rounded-md border border-border bg-muted"
                  >
                    <img
                      src={item.preview}
                      alt={item.file.name}
                      className="h-full w-full object-cover"
                    />
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => removeFile(item.id)}
                      className="absolute right-2 top-2 flex h-7 w-7 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-destructive text-white opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"
                      aria-label="删除图片"
                    >
                      <Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}

            <div className="mt-6 flex flex-col gap-3 md:flex-row">
              <Button
                onClick={handleRecognize}
                disabled={isRecognizing || files.length === 0}
                className="w-full md:w-auto"
              >
                {isRecognizing ? (
                  <Loader2 className="mr-2 h-4 w-4 animate-spin" />
                ) : (
                  <ScanLine className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />
                )}
                {isRecognizing ? "识别中…" : "开始识别"}
              </Button>

              <Button
                variant="secondary"
                onClick={handleExport}
                disabled={!hasResults || isRecognizing}
                className="w-full md:w-auto"
              >
                <FileDown className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />
                导出表格
              </Button>
            </div>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>

        {hasResults && (
          <section className="mt-8 md:mt-12">
            <h2 className="mb-4 text-lg font-medium tracking-tight">识别结果</h2>
            <Card className="border border-border bg-card overflow-hidden">
              <div className="w-full overflow-x-auto">
                <Table>
                  <TableHeader>
                    <TableRow>
                      <TableHead className="whitespace-nowrap">单词</TableHead>
                      <TableHead className="whitespace-nowrap">音标</TableHead>
                      <TableHead className="whitespace-nowrap">中译</TableHead>
                    </TableRow>
                  </TableHeader>
                  <TableBody>{tableRows}</TableBody>
                </Table>
              </div>
            </Card>
          </section>
        )}

        {!hasResults && !isRecognizing && (
          <div className="mt-12 flex flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-lg border border-dashed border-border bg-secondary/20 py-12 text-center">
            <FileImage className="h-10 w-10 text-muted-foreground" />
            <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-muted-foreground">上传图片并点击「开始识别」即可生成表格</p>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default WordOcrPage;

```

Old String: ```
import { compressImage } from "./compressImage";

/**
 * 将 File 对象转换为纯 Base64 字符串（不含 data URL 前缀）。
 * 先尝试对图片进行等比压缩以减小请求体积；若压缩失败则回退到原始文件。
 * 采用分块读取，避免大图片触发 RangeError 或请求体超限。
 */
export async function fileToBase64(file: File): Promise<string> {
  let target: File | Blob = file;
  try {
    target = await compressImage(file);
  } catch (compressErr) {
    console.warn("图片压缩失败，回退到原始文件", compressErr);
  }

  const buffer = await target.arrayBuffer();
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(buffer);
  const chunkSize = 8192;
  let binary = "";
  for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i += chunkSize) {
    binary += String.fromCharCode(...bytes.subarray(i, i + chunkSize));
  }
  return btoa(binary);
}
```
New String: ```
import { compressImage } from "./compressImage";

/**
 * 将 File 对象转换为纯 Base64 字符串（不含 data URL 前缀）。
 * 先对图片进行等比压缩以减小请求体积；压缩失败直接抛出异常，避免
 * 将不支持的格式（如 iOS HEIC）直接传给后端导致报错。
 * 采用分块读取，避免大图片触发 RangeError 或请求体超限。
 */
export async function fileToBase64(file: File): Promise<string> {
  const compressed = await compressImage(file);

  const buffer = await compressed.arrayBuffer();
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(buffer);
  const chunkSize = 8192;
  let binary = "";
  for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i += chunkSize) {
    binary += String.fromCharCode(...bytes.subarray(i, i + chunkSize));
  }
  return btoa(binary);
}
```

     1 /**
     2  * 从 OCR 识别出的原始文本中提取英文单词。
     3  * 按空格、换行、常见标点分割，过滤非字母字符并统一小写。
     4  */
     5 export function extractEnglishWords(texts: string[]): string[] {
     6   const words: string[] = [];
     7   for (const text of texts) {
     8     const segments = text
     9       .replace(/[\r\n]+/g, " ")
    10       .replace(/[^a-zA-Z\s'-]/g, " ")
    11       .split(/\s+/);
    12     for (const segment of segments) {
    13       const cleaned = segment
    14         .replace(/^[^a-zA-Z]+|[^a-zA-Z]+$/g, "")
    15         .toLowerCase();
    16       if (cleaned.length > 0 && /^[a-zA-Z'-]+$/.test(cleaned)) {
    17         words.push(cleaned);
    18       }
    19     }
    20   }
    21   return words;
    22 }
