    46       });
    47     }
    48     if (newFiles.length > 0) {
    49       setFiles((prev) => [...prev, ...newFiles]);
    50     }
    51   }, []);
    52 
    53   const onDropRejected = useCallback((fileRejections: FileRejection[]) => {
    54     for (const rejection of fileRejections) {
    55       const errors = rejection.errors.map((e) => e.message).join("；");
    56       toast.error(`文件「${rejection.file.name}」被拒绝：${errors}`);
    57     }
    58   }, []);
    59 
    60   const { getRootProps, getInputProps, isDragActive, fileRejections } = useDropzone({
    61     onDrop,
    62     onDropRejected,
    63     accept: {
    64       "image/*": [".jpeg", ".jpg", ".png", ".bmp", ".webp"],
    65     },
    66     multiple: true,
    67   });
    68 
    69   const removeFile = useCallback((id: string) => {
    70     setFiles((prev) => {
    71       const target = prev.find((f) => f.id === id);
    72       if (target) URL.revokeObjectURL(target.preview);
    73       return prev.filter((f) => f.id !== id);
    74     });
    75   }, []);
    76 
    77   const handleRecognize = useCallback(async () => {
    78     if (files.length === 0) {
    79       toast.error("请先上传至少一张图片");
    80       return;
    81     }
    82 
    83     setIsRecognizing(true);
    84     setResults([]);
    85     const toastId = toast.loading("正在识别图片中的单词，请稍候…");
    86 
    87     const allWords: string[] = [];
    88     let failedCount = 0;
    89 
    90     try {
    91       for (const item of files) {
    92         try {
    93           console.log(`[OCR] 开始处理图片: ${item.file.name}`);
    94           const base64 = await fileToBase64(item.file);
    95           console.log(`[OCR] 图片转 Base64 完成, 长度: ${base64.length}`);
    96           const ocrResult = await recognizeImage(base64);
    97           console.log(`[OCR] 识别结果:`, ocrResult);
    98           const texts = ocrResult.words_result.map((w) => w.words);
    99           const words = extractEnglishWords(texts);
   100           console.log(`[OCR] 提取单词 [${item.file.name}]:`, words);
   101           allWords.push(...words);
   102         } catch (itemErr) {
   103           failedCount++;
   104           console.error(`[OCR] 图片「${item.file.name}」处理异常:`, itemErr);
   105           const itemMessage = itemErr instanceof Error ? itemErr.message : "未知错误";
   106           toast.error(`图片「${item.file.name}」识别失败：${itemMessage}`);
   107         }
   108       }
   109 
   110       if (allWords.length === 0) {
   111         if (failedCount > 0) {
   112           toast.error("所有图片均识别失败，请检查图片内容或网络后重试", { id: toastId });
   113         } else {
   114           toast.success("图片中未识别到任何英文单词，请确认图片包含英语内容", { id: toastId });
   115         }
   116         return;
   117       }
   118 
   119       if (failedCount > 0) {
   120         toast.warning(`${failedCount} 张图片识别失败，已跳过`, { id: toastId });
   121       } else {
   122         toast.loading(`已识别 ${allWords.length} 个单词，正在查询释义…`, { id: toastId });
   123       }
   124 
   125       const wordInfos = await fetchWordInfo(allWords);
   126       console.log(`[OCR] 单词信息查询完成:`, wordInfos);
   127 
   128       setResults(wordInfos);
   129       toast.success(`成功识别并查询 ${wordInfos.length} 个单词`, { id: toastId });
   130     } catch (err) {
   131       console.error("[OCR] 整体流程异常:", err);
   132       const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "识别过程出现未知错误";
   133       toast.error(message, { id: toastId });
   134     } finally {
   135       setIsRecognizing(false);
   136     }
   137   }, [files]);
   138 
   139   const handleExport = useCallback(() => {
   140     if (results.length === 0) {
   141       toast.error("暂无可导出的识别结果");
   142       return;
   143     }
   144     exportWordInfoToCsv(results);
   145     toast.success("表格已导出");
   146   }, [results]);
   147 
   148   const hasResults = results.length > 0;
   149 
   150   const tableRows = useMemo(
   151     () =>
   152       results.map((item, index) => (
   153         <TableRow key={`${item.word}-${index}`}>
   154           <TableCell className="font-medium">{item.word}</TableCell>
   155           <TableCell className="text-muted-foreground font-mono">{item.phonetic || "-"}</TableCell>
   156           <TableCell>{item.translation || "-"}</TableCell>
   157         </TableRow>
   158       )),
   159     [results]
   160   );
   161 
   162   return (
   163     <div className="min-h-screen w-full bg-background text-foreground">
   164       <div className="mx-auto max-w-5xl px-4 py-8 md:py-12">
   165         <header className="mb-8 md:mb-12">
   166           <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight md:text-3xl text-balance">
   167             单词图片识别工具
   168           </h1>
   169           <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground md:text-base max-w-prose">
   170             上传包含英语单词的图片，自动识别内容并生成「单词 / 音标 / 中译」表格。
   171           </p>
   172         </header>
   173 
   174         <Card className="border border-border bg-card">
   175           <CardContent className="p-4 md:p-6">
   176             <div
   177               {...getRootProps()}
   178               className={`
   179                 cursor-pointer rounded-lg border border-dashed border-border bg-secondary/30
   180                 px-4 py-8 text-center transition-colors hover:bg-secondary/60 md:py-12
   181                 ${isDragActive ? "border-primary bg-primary/5" : ""}
   182               `}
   183             >
   184               <input {...getInputProps()} />
   185               <div className="mx-auto flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-muted">
   186                 <Upload className="h-5 w-5 text-muted-foreground" />
   187               </div>
   188               <p className="mt-4 text-sm font-medium">拖拽图片到此处，或点击选择图片</p>
   189               <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">支持 JPG、PNG、BMP、WebP，单张不超过 10MB</p>
   190             </div>
   191 
   192             {files.length > 0 && (
   193               <div className="mt-6 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3 md:grid-cols-4">
   194                 {files.map((item) => (
   195                   <div
   196                     key={item.id}
   197                     className="group relative aspect-square overflow-hidden rounded-md border border-border bg-muted"
   198                   >
   199                     <img
   200                       src={item.preview}
   201                       alt={item.file.name}
   202                       className="h-full w-full object-cover"
   203                     />
   204                     <Button
   205                       type="button"
   206                       variant="destructive"
   207                       size="icon"
   208                       onClick={() => removeFile(item.id)}
   209                       className="absolute right-2 top-2 h-7 w-7 rounded-full opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"
   210                       aria-label="删除图片"
   211                     >
   212                       <Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
   213                     </Button>
   214                   </div>
   215                 ))}
   216               </div>
   217             )}
   218 
   219             <div className="mt-6 flex flex-col gap-3 md:flex-row">
   220               <Button
   221                 onClick={handleRecognize}
   222                 disabled={isRecognizing || files.length === 0}
   223                 className="w-full md:w-auto"
   224               >
   225                 {isRecognizing ? (
   226                   <Loader2 className="mr-2 h-4 w-4 animate-spin" />
   227                 ) : (
   228                   <ScanLine className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />
   229                 )}
   230                 {isRecognizing ? "识别中…" : "开始识别"}
   231               </Button>
   232 
   233               <Button
   234                 variant="secondary"
   235                 onClick={handleExport}
   236                 disabled={!hasResults || isRecognizing}
   237                 className="w-full md:w-auto"
   238               >
   239                 <FileDown className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />
   240                 导出表格
   241               </Button>
   242             </div>
   243           </CardContent>
   244         </Card>
   245 
   246         {hasResults && (
   247           <section className="mt-8 md:mt-12">
   248             <h2 className="mb-4 text-lg font-medium tracking-tight">识别结果</h2>
   249             <Card className="border border-border bg-card overflow-hidden">
   250               <div className="w-full overflow-x-auto">
   251                 <Table>
   252                   <TableHeader>
   253                     <TableRow>
   254                       <TableHead className="whitespace-nowrap">单词</TableHead>
   255                       <TableHead className="whitespace-nowrap">音标</TableHead>
   256                       <TableHead className="whitespace-nowrap">中译</TableHead>
   257                     </TableRow>
   258                   </TableHeader>
   259                   <TableBody>{tableRows}</TableBody>
   260                 </Table>
   261               </div>
   262             </Card>
   263           </section>
   264         )}
   265 
   266         {!hasResults && !isRecognizing && (
   267           <div className="mt-12 flex flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-lg border border-dashed border-border bg-secondary/20 py-12 text-center">
   268             <FileImage className="h-10 w-10 text-muted-foreground" />
   269             <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-muted-foreground">上传图片并点击「开始识别」即可生成表格</p>
   270           </div>
   271         )}
   272       </div>
   273     </div>
   274   );
   275 };
   276 
   277 export default WordOcrPage;
   278 


</observation>

python3 -c "
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFont
import io, base64
img = Image.new('RGB', (400, 100), color='white')
draw = ImageDraw.Draw(img)
try:
    font = ImageFont.truetype('/usr/share/fonts/truetype/dejavu/DejaVuSans-Bold.ttf', 32)
except:
    font = ImageFont.load_default()
draw.text((20, 30), 'hello world apple', fill='black', font=font)
buf = io.BytesIO()
img.save(buf, format='PNG')
with open('/tmp/demo_clean.txt', 'w') as f:
    f.write(base64.b64encode(buf.getvalue()).decode('utf-8'))
print('done', len(base64.b64encode(buf.getvalue()).decode('utf-8')))
"
</action>

Old String: ```
    setIsRecognizing(true);
    setResults([]);
    const toastId = toast.loading("正在识别图片中的单词，请稍候…");

    const allWords: string[] = [];
    let failedCount = 0;

    try {
      for (const item of files) {
        try {
          console.log(`[OCR] 开始处理图片: ${item.file.name}`);
          const base64 = await fileToBase64(item.file);
          console.log(`[OCR] 图片转 Base64 完成, 长度: ${base64.length}`);
          const ocrResult = await recognizeImage(base64);
          console.log(`[OCR] 识别结果:`, ocrResult);
          const texts = ocrResult.words_result.map((w) => w.words);
          const words = extractEnglishWords(texts);
          console.log(`[OCR] 提取单词 [${item.file.name}]:`, words);
          allWords.push(...words);
        } catch (itemErr) {
          failedCount++;
          console.error(`[OCR] 图片「${item.file.name}」处理异常:`, itemErr);
          const itemMessage = itemErr instanceof Error ? itemErr.message : "未知错误";
          toast.error(`图片「${item.file.name}」识别失败：${itemMessage}`);
        }
      }

      if (allWords.length === 0) {
        if (failedCount > 0) {
          toast.error("所有图片均识别失败，请检查图片内容或网络后重试", { id: toastId });
        } else {
          toast.success("图片中未识别到任何英文单词，请确认图片包含英语内容", { id: toastId });
        }
        return;
      }

      if (failedCount > 0) {
        toast.warning(`${failedCount} 张图片识别失败，已跳过`, { id: toastId });
      } else {
        toast.loading(`已识别 ${allWords.length} 个单词，正在查询释义…`, { id: toastId });
      }

      const wordInfos = await fetchWordInfo(allWords);
      console.log(`[OCR] 单词信息查询完成:`, wordInfos);

      setResults(wordInfos);
      toast.success(`成功识别并查询 ${wordInfos.length} 个单词`, { id: toastId });
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("[OCR] 整体流程异常:", err);
      const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "识别过程出现未知错误";
      toast.error(message, { id: toastId });
    } finally {
      setIsRecognizing(false);
    }
  }, [files]);
```
New String: ```
    setIsRecognizing(true);
    setResults([]);
    setRawTexts([]);
    const toastId = toast.loading("正在识别图片中的单词，请稍候…");

    const allWords: string[] = [];
    const allRawTexts: string[] = [];
    let failedCount = 0;

    try {
      for (const item of files) {
        try {
          console.log(`[OCR] 开始处理图片: ${item.file.name}`);
          const base64 = await fileToBase64(item.file);
          console.log(`[OCR] 图片转 Base64 完成, 长度: ${base64.length}`);
          const ocrResult = await recognizeImage(base64);
          console.log(`[OCR] 识别结果:`, ocrResult);
          const texts = ocrResult.words_result.map((w) => w.words);
          allRawTexts.push(...texts);
          const words = extractEnglishWords(texts);
          console.log(`[OCR] 提取单词 [${item.file.name}]:`, words);
          allWords.push(...words);
        } catch (itemErr) {
          failedCount++;
          console.error(`[OCR] 图片「${item.file.name}」处理异常:`, itemErr);
          const itemMessage = itemErr instanceof Error ? itemErr.message : "未知错误";
          toast.error(`图片「${item.file.name}」识别失败：${itemMessage}`);
        }
      }

      setRawTexts(allRawTexts);

      if (allWords.length === 0) {
        if (failedCount > 0) {
          toast.error("所有图片均识别失败，请检查图片内容或网络后重试", { id: toastId });
        } else {
          toast.success("图片中未识别到任何英文单词，请确认图片包含英语内容", { id: toastId });
        }
        return;
      }

      if (failedCount > 0) {
        toast.warning(`${failedCount} 张图片识别失败，已跳过`, { id: toastId });
      } else {
        toast.loading(`已识别 ${allWords.length} 个单词，正在查询释义…`, { id: toastId });
      }

      const wordInfos = await fetchWordInfo(allWords);
      console.log(`[OCR] 单词信息查询完成:`, wordInfos);

      setResults(wordInfos);
      toast.success(`成功识别并查询 ${wordInfos.length} 个单词`, { id: toastId });
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("[OCR] 整体流程异常:", err);
      const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "识别过程出现未知错误";
      toast.error(message, { id: toastId });
    } finally {
      setIsRecognizing(false);
    }
  }, [files]);

  const handleDemoTest = useCallback(async () => {
    setIsRecognizing(true);
    setResults([]);
    setRawTexts([]);
    const toastId = toast.loading("正在使用示例图片测试 OCR 链路…");

    try {
      const ocrResult = await recognizeImage(DEMO_IMAGE_BASE64);
      console.log("[OCR] 示例测试识别结果:", ocrResult);
      const texts = ocrResult.words_result.map((w) => w.words);
      setRawTexts(texts);
      const words = extractEnglishWords(texts);
      console.log("[OCR] 示例提取单词:", words);

      if (words.length === 0) {
        toast.success("示例图片未提取到英文单词，但 OCR 识别正常", { id: toastId });
        return;
      }

      toast.loading(`示例已识别 ${words.length} 个单词，正在查询释义…`, { id: toastId });
      const wordInfos = await fetchWordInfo(words);
      setResults(wordInfos);
      toast.success(`示例测试成功：共 ${wordInfos.length} 个单词`, { id: toastId });
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("[OCR] 示例测试异常:", err);
      const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "示例测试失败";
      toast.error(message, { id: toastId });
    } finally {
      setIsRecognizing(false);
    }
  }, []);
```

COMMAND:
python3 -c "
import base64
with open('/workspace/app-cui721lyjt35/src/utils/demoImage.ts') as f:
    content = f.read()
# Extract base64 string between quotes
import re
m = re.search(r'\"([^\"]+)\"', content)
if m:
    b64 = m.group(1)
    print('base64 length:', len(b64))
    try:
        data = base64.b64decode(b64)
        print('decoded length:', len(data))
        # Check if it's a valid PNG
        if data[:8] == b'\\x89PNG\\r\\n\\x1a\\n':
            print('Valid PNG header')
        else:
            print('Invalid PNG header:', data[:8].hex())
    except Exception as e:
        print('decode error:', e)
else:
    print('no base64 found')
"
